Conclusion: 纯合成数据在图形设计分解任务中是可行且有效的可扩展方案，有望被更广泛地采纳为分层设计编辑系统的实用基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in image generation have made it easy to produce high-quality images. However, these outputs are inherently flattened, entangling foreground elements, background, and text within a fixed canvas. As a result, flexible post-generation editing remains challenging, revealing a clear last-mile gap toward practical usability. Existing approaches either rely on scarce proprietary layered assets or construct partially synthetic data from limited structural priors. However, both strategies face fundamental challenges in scalability. In this work, we investigate whether pure synthetic layered data can improve graphic design decomposition. We make the assumption that, in graphic design, effective decomposition does not require modeling inter-layer dependencies as precisely as in natural-image composition, since design elements are often intentionally arranged as modular and semantically separable components. Concretely, we conduct a data-centric study based on CLD baseline, which is a state-of-the-art layer decomposition framework. Based on the baseline, we construct our own synthetic dataset, SynLayers, generate textual supervision using vision language models, and automate inference inputs with VLM-predicted bounding boxes. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) even training with purely synthetic data can outperform non-scalable alternatives such as the widely used PrismLayersPro dataset, demonstrating its viability as a scalable and effective substitute; (2) performance consistently improves with increased training data scale, while gains begin to saturate at around 50K samples; and (3) synthetic data enables balanced control over layer-count distributions, avoiding the layer-count imbalance commonly observed in real-world datasets. We hope this data-centric study encourages broader adoption of synthetic data as a practical foundation for layered design editing systems.

</details>


### [145] [SANA-WM: Efficient Minute-Scale World Modeling with Hybrid Linear Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15178)
*Haoyi Zhu,Haozhe Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Tian Ye,Junsong Chen,Jincheng Yu,Tong He,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SANA-WM是一个高效的26亿参数开源世界模型，原生支持一分钟720p视频生成，具有精确相机控制能力。它采用混合线性注意力、双分支相机控制、两阶段生成流水线和鲁棒标注流程四大核心设计，在仅用21.3万视频片段、15天训练、单GPU即可生成60秒视频的条件下，实现了与大规模工业基线相当的视觉质量，且吞吐量提升36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在长时视频生成（分钟级）方面存在效率低、相机控制不精确、训练数据需求大等问题，缺乏高效且开源的长视频世界模型。

Method: (1) 混合线性注意力：帧级门控DeltaNet结合softmax注意力，实现内存高效的长上下文建模；(2) 双分支相机控制：确保精确的6自由度轨迹遵循；(3) 两阶段生成流水线：长视频精炼器提升阶段一输出的质量和一致性；(4) 鲁棒标注流程：从公开视频中提取精确的公制尺度6自由度相机姿态，生成高质量动作标签。

Result: 仅用约21.3万视频片段，在64块H100上训练15天，单GPU生成60秒720p视频；蒸馏变体可在单张RTX 5090上运行，量化后34秒完成60秒720p视频去噪；视觉质量与LingBot-World和HY-WorldPlay等大规模基线相当，动作跟随准确率优于现有开源基线，吞吐量提升36倍。

Conclusion: SANA-WM证明了通过精心设计的架构和高效的数据标注流程，可以在开源条件下实现与大规模工业级模型相当的分钟级视频生成质量，同时大幅提升效率，为可扩展的世界建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce SANA-WM, an efficient 2.6B-parameter open-source world model natively trained for one-minute generation, synthesizing high-fidelity, 720p, minute-scale videos with precise camera control. SANA-WM achieves visual quality comparable to large-scale industrial baselines such as LingBot-World and HY-WorldPlay, while significantly improving efficiency. Four core designs drive our architecture: (1) Hybrid Linear Attention combines frame-wise Gated DeltaNet (GDN) with softmax attention for memory-efficient long-context modeling. (2) Dual-Branch Camera Control ensures precise 6-DoF trajectory adherence. (3) Two-Stage Generation Pipeline applies a long-video refiner to stage-1 outputs, improving quality and consistency across sequences. (4) Robust Annotation Pipeline extracts accurate metric-scale 6-DoF camera poses from public videos to yield high-quality, spatiotemporally consistent action labels. Driven by these designs, SANA-WMdemonstrates remarkable efficiency across data, training compute, and inference hardware: it uses only $\sim$213K public video clips with metric-scale pose supervision, completes training in 15 days on 64 H100s, and generates each 60s clip on a single GPU; its distilled variant can be deployed on a single RTX 5090 with NVFP4 quantization to denoise a 60s 720p clip in 34s. On our one-minute world-model benchmark, SANA-WM demonstrates stronger action-following accuracy than prior open-source baselines and achieves comparable visual quality at $36\times$ higher throughput for scalable world modeling.

</details>


### [146] [From Plans to Pixels: Learning to Plan and Orchestrate for Open-Ended Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15181)
*Anirudh Sundara Rajan,Krishna Kumar Singh,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个基于经验驱动（experiential）的长期图像编辑框架，通过规划器（planner）分解复杂指令、编排器（orchestrator）选择工具和区域执行每一步、视觉语言评判器（judge）提供基于结果的奖励，并利用奖励训练编排器、用成功轨迹优化规划器，从而在抽象多步编辑任务上优于单步或规则基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型在处理抽象、多步指令时表现不佳，而基于智能体的方法依赖手工管道或教师模仿，限制了灵活性，且学习与实际编辑效果脱节。

Method: 提出经验驱动框架：规划器将复杂指令分解为原子步骤；编排器为每一步选择工具和编辑区域；视觉语言评判器根据指令遵循度和视觉质量提供基于结果的奖励；编排器通过最大化奖励进行训练，成功轨迹用于优化规划器。

Result: 在抽象多步编辑任务上，该方法比单步或基于规则的多步基线生成更连贯、更可靠的编辑结果。

Conclusion: 将规划与奖励驱动的执行紧密耦合，能显著提升图像编辑在复杂指令下的效果。

Abstract: Modern image editing models produce realistic results but struggle with abstract, multi step instructions (e.g., ``make this advertisement more vegetarian-friendly''). Prior agent based methods decompose such tasks but rely on handcrafted pipelines or teacher imitation, limiting flexibility and decoupling learning from actual editing outcomes. We propose an experiential framework for long-horizon image editing, where a planner generates structured atomic decompositions and an orchestrator selects tools and regions to execute each step. A vision language judge provides outcome-based rewards for instruction adherence and visual quality. The orchestrator is trained to maximize these rewards, and successful trajectories are used to refine the planner. By tightly coupling planning with reward driven execution, our approach yields more coherent and reliable edits than single-step or rule-based multistep baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Warp-as-History: Generalizable Camera-Controlled Video Generation from One Training Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15182)
*Yifan Wang,Tong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Warp-as-History接口，通过将相机运动产生的图像扭曲作为伪历史帧输入冻结的视频生成模型，无需训练或测试时优化即可实现零样本的相机轨迹控制视频生成，轻量级LoRA微调可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机控制视频生成方法通常需要对大规模标注数据进行后训练，或依赖测试时优化，成本高昂；训练无关方法虽免去后训练，但将成本转移到测试阶段。缺乏一种既无需训练又无需测试时优化的简单接口。

Method: 提出Warp-as-History接口：1) 根据目标相机轨迹，将历史帧进行相机扭曲生成伪历史帧；2) 将伪历史帧与目标帧的位置编码对齐；3) 移除无效的扭曲历史令牌（无有效源观测的部分）。通过模型的视觉历史路径输入，无需任何训练、架构修改或测试时优化。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验证实该方法有效：零样本下冻结模型即可跟随相机轨迹；轻量级离线LoRA微调（仅需一个相机标注视频）可进一步提升相机遵从度、视觉质量和运动动态，且泛化到未见视频。

Conclusion: Warp-as-History提供了一个简单、高效且无需额外训练的相机控制接口，揭示了冻结视频生成模型的零样本相机控制能力，同时轻量级微调可进一步增强效果。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation has made substantial progress, enabling generated videos to follow prescribed viewpoint trajectories. However, existing methods usually learn camera-specific conditioning through camera encoders, control branches, or attention and positional-encoding modifications, which often require post-training on large-scale camera-annotated videos. Training-free alternatives avoid such post-training, but often shift the cost to test-time optimization or extra denoising-time guidance. We propose Warp-as-History, a simple interface that turns camera-induced warps into camera-warped pseudo-history with target-frame positional alignment and visible-token selection. Given a target camera trajectory, we construct camera-warped pseudo-history from past observations and feed it through the model's visual-history pathway. Crucially, we align its positional encoding with the target frames being denoised and remove warped-history tokens without valid source observations. Without any training, architectural modification, or test-time optimization, this interface reveals a non-trivial zero-shot capability of a frozen video generation model to follow camera trajectories. Moreover, lightweight offline LoRA finetuning on only one camera-annotated video further improves this capability and generalizes to unseen videos, improving camera adherence, visual quality, and motion dynamics without test-time optimization or target-video adaptation. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets confirm the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [148] [RAVEN: Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation with Consistency-model GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15190)
*Yanzuo Lu,Ronglai Zuo,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RAVEN框架（训练时测试框架）和CM-GRPO（一致性模型组相对策略优化），解决因果自回归视频扩散模型在长时域生成中因训练-推理历史分布不一致导致的质量下降问题，并首次将强化学习直接应用于一致性采样核，提升视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果自回归视频扩散模型虽支持实时流式生成，但训练时的历史分布与推理时的历史分布之间存在持续差距，限制了长时域生成质量。需要一种方法对齐训练与推理时的注意力机制，并让下游损失信号反向传播到历史表示上。

Method: 1. **RAVEN**：在训练时将自回归模型的自我推理结果重新打包成交替的干净历史端点与噪声去噪状态序列，使训练注意力与推理时外推对齐，并允许下游块损失监督历史表示。2. **CM-GRPO**：将一致性模型采样步重新表述为条件高斯转移，直接在该核上应用在线强化学习（PPO），避免了先前流模型RL方法中的Euler-Maruyama辅助过程。

Result: RAVEN在质量、语义和动态程度评估上超越了现有因果视频蒸馏基线；结合CM-GRPO可获得进一步提升。

Conclusion: RAVEN通过训练-测试对齐有效缩小了因果自回归模型的长时域生成质量差距；CM-GRPO提供了一种更直接的RL训练范式，可无缝集成到一致性模型框架中。

Abstract: Causal autoregressive video diffusion models support real-time streaming generation by extrapolating future chunks from previously generated content. Distilling such generators from high-fidelity bidirectional teachers yields competitive few-step models, yet a persistent gap between the history distributions encountered during training and those arising at inference constrains generation quality over long horizons. We introduce the Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation Network (RAVEN), a training-time test framework that repacks each self rollout into an interleaved sequence of clean historical endpoints and noisy denoising states. This formulation aligns training attention with inference-time extrapolation and allows downstream chunk losses to supervise the history representations on which future predictions depend. We further propose Consistency-model Group Relative Policy Optimization (CM-GRPO), which reformulates a consistency sampling step as a conditional Gaussian transition and applies online Reinforcement Learning (RL) directly to this kernel, avoiding the Euler-Maruyama auxiliary process adopted in prior flow-model RL formulations. Experiments demonstrate that RAVEN surpasses recent causal video distillation baselines across quality, semantic, and dynamic degree evaluations, and that CM-GRPO provides further gains when combined with RAVEN.

</details>


### [149] [Aligning Latent Geometry for Spherical Flow Matching in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15193)
*Tuna Han Salih Meral,Kaan Oktay,Hidir Yesiltepe,Adil Kaan Akan,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种球形潜空间流匹配方法，通过将图像潜编码的径向分量固定、使用球面线性插值替代欧几里得线性插值，使得生成路径始终保持在球面上，从而在无额外模块或目标函数的情况下提升ImageNet-256的FID分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配方法中，高斯噪声和VAE潜变量都集中在薄球壳上，而欧几里得线性路径会离开这些球壳，导致生成效率和质量下降。作者通过分量交换探针实验发现语义内容主要由方向（角度）携带，半径贡献很小，因此希望设计一种始终保持在球面上的生成路径。

Method: 1) 将潜变量分解为径向和角度分量；2) 将数据潜变量的半径固定为统一值，高斯噪声也做径向投影作为球形先验；3) 冻结编码器，微调解码器以适应固定半径；4) 用球面线性插值替代线性插值，确保路径始终在球面上；5) 速度目标仅为角度分量。

Result: 在相同训练条件下，该方法在不同图像分词器上均一致地提升了类条件ImageNet-256的FID分数，且不改变扩散架构、无需额外编码器或表示对齐目标。

Conclusion: 球形潜空间流匹配是一种简单有效的改进方法，通过纯几何修正（径向固定+球面插值）即可提升图像生成质量，验证了潜空间中方向承载语义、半径贡献有限的洞见。

Abstract: Latent flow matching for image generation usually transports Gaussian noise to variational autoencoder latents along linear paths. Both endpoints, however, concentrate in thin spherical shells, and a Euclidean chord leaves those shells even when preprocessing aligns their radii. By decomposing each latent token into radial and angular components, we show through component-swap probes that decoded perceptual and semantic content is carried predominantly by direction, with radius contributing much less. We therefore project data latents onto a fixed token radius, use the radial projection of Gaussian noise as the spherical prior, finetune the decoder with the encoder frozen, and replace linear interpolation with spherical linear interpolation. The resulting geodesic paths stay on the sphere at every timestep, and their velocity targets are purely angular by construction. Under matched training, the method consistently improves class-conditional ImageNet-256 FID across different image tokenizers, leaves the diffusion architecture unchanged, and requires no auxiliary encoder or representation-alignment objective.

</details>


### [150] [VGGT-$Ω$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15195)
*Jianyuan Wang,Minghao Chen,Shangzhan Zhang,Nikita Karaev,Johannes Schönberger,Patrick Labatut,Piotr Bojanowski,David Novotny,Andrea Vedaldi,Christian Rupprecht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VGGT-Ω，通过架构简化、训练效率提升和自监督学习，在静态和动态场景重建中显著超越前代模型，并证明重建质量随模型和数据规模可预测地提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期前馈重建模型（如VGGT）虽已具备竞争力，但尚未系统研究其质量与模型及数据规模之间的扩展规律，且训练效率有限，难以在大规模数据上训练。

Method: 1) 简化架构：用单一密集预测头替代多任务头，移除高分辨率卷积层；2) 引入register机制及register attention，将跨帧信息交换限制在register上，替代全局注意力；3) 构建高质量动态场景数据标注流程；4) 结合自监督学习协议，利用大量无标签视频数据。

Result: 训练显存仅为前代的30%，可使用15倍于前人的监督数据和大量无标签视频；在Sintel上相机估计精度提升77%；多个静态/动态场景基准上达到最优。

Conclusion: 重建可以作为一种可扩展的、强大的空间理解代理任务；学习到的register还可用于视觉-语言-动作模型及语言对齐。

Abstract: Recent feed-forward reconstruction models, such as VGGT, have proven competitive with traditional optimization-based reconstructors while also providing geometry-aware features useful for other tasks. Here, we show that the quality of these models scales predictably with model and data size. We do so by introducing VGGT-$Ω$, which substantially improves reconstruction accuracy, efficiency, and capabilities for both static and dynamic scenes. To enable training this model at an unprecedented scale, we introduce architectural changes that improve training efficiency, a high-quality data annotation pipeline that supports dynamic scenes, and a self-supervised learning protocol. We simplify VGGT's architecture by using a single dense prediction head with multi-task supervision and removing the expensive high-resolution convolutional layers. We also use registers to aggregate scene information into a compact representation and introduce register attention, which restricts inter-frame information exchange to these registers, in part replacing global attention. In this way, during training, VGGT-$Ω$ uses only about 30% of the GPU memory of its predecessor, allowing us to train with 15x more supervised data than prior work and to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled video data. VGGT-$Ω$ achieves strong results for reconstruction of static and dynamic scenes across multiple benchmarks, for example, improving over the previous best camera estimation accuracy on Sintel by 77%. We also show that the learned registers can improve vision-language-action models and support alignment with language, suggesting that reconstruction can be a powerful and scalable proxy task for spatial understanding. Project Page: http://vggt-omega.github.io/

</details>


### [151] [RefDecoder: Enhancing Visual Generation with Conditional Video Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15196)
*Xiang Fan,Yuheng Wang,Bohan Fang,Zhongzheng Ren,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视频生成中VAE解码器缺少条件控制导致细节丢失和结构不一致的问题，提出RefDecoder，通过参考注意力机制将高保真参考图像信号注入解码过程，无需额外微调即可替换现有系统，在多项基准上提升PSNR达+2.1dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现行潜在扩散模型中，去噪网络受到强条件控制，但解码器通常是无条件的，这种架构不对称性导致输出相对于输入图像存在细节丢失和结构不一致。

Method: 提出RefDecoder，通过轻量级图像编码器将参考帧映射为细节丰富的高维token，在解码器每个上采样阶段通过参考注意力机制与去噪后的视频潜在token协同处理，实现高保真参考图像信号的直接注入。

Result: 在Inter4K、WebVid和Large Motion重建基准上，相比无条件基线PSNR提升最高+2.1dB；在VBench I2V基准上，主体一致性、背景一致性和整体质量分数均有全面提升；在风格迁移和视频编辑细化等任务上也表现良好。

Conclusion: 解码器同样需要条件控制才能保持结构完整性；RefDecoder可直接替换现有视频生成系统而无需额外微调，具有广泛的通用性。

Abstract: Video generation powers a vast array of downstream applications. However, while the de facto standard, i.e., latent diffusion models, typically employ heavily conditioned denoising networks, their decoders often remain unconditional. We observe that this architectural asymmetry leads to significant loss of detail and inconsistency relative to the input image. To address this, we argue that the decoder requires equal conditioning to preserve structural integrity. We introduce RefDecoder, a reference-conditioned video VAE decoder by injecting high-fidelity reference image signal directly into the decoding process via reference attention. Specifically, a lightweight image encoder maps the reference frame into the detail-rich high-dimensional tokens, which are co-processed with the denoised video latent tokens at each decoder up-sampling stage. We demonstrate consistent improvements across several distinct decoder backbones (e.g., Wan 2.1 and VideoVAE+), achieving up to +2.1dB PSNR over the unconditional baselines on the Inter4K, WebVid, and Large Motion reconstruction benchmarks. Notably, RefDecoder can be directly swapped into existing video generation systems without additional fine-tuning, and we report across-the-board improvements in subject consistency, background consistency, and overall quality scores on the VBench I2V benchmark. Beyond I2V, RefDecoder generalizes well to a wide range of visual generation tasks such as style transfer and video editing refinement.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [GraphBit: A Graph-based Agentic Framework for Non-Linear Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13848)
*Yeahia Sarker,Md Rahmat Ullah,Musa Molla,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphBit 是一个基于引擎编排的框架，通过有向无环图（DAG）定义工作流，解决了提示编排中幻觉、死循环和不可复现问题。在GAIA基准测试中，以67.6%准确率、零幻觉、11.9ms延迟和最高吞吐量超越六种现有框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示编排的Agentic LLM框架存在幻觉式路由、无限循环和执行不可复现的问题，亟需一种确定性、可审计的框架来保证工作流的可靠性。

Method: GraphBit 采用引擎编排方式，将工作流定义为有向无环图（DAG），Agent作为类型化函数运行；基于Rust的引擎负责路由、状态转换和工具调用，支持并行分支执行、条件控制流和可配置错误恢复；设计了包含临时暂存空间、结构化状态和外部连接器的三层记忆架构，隔离各阶段上下文。

Result: 在GAIA基准测试中，GraphBit达到67.6%的最高准确率，零框架诱导幻觉，最低延迟11.9ms，最高吞吐量。消融实验表明每层记忆架构均有贡献，确定性执行在工具密集型任务上效果最显著。

Conclusion: GraphBit通过将工作流从提示驱动转为引擎驱动的DAG确定性执行，有效解决了LLM框架的核心可靠性缺陷，为生产环境部署提供了一个可控、可复现且高性能的方案。

Abstract: Agentic LLM frameworks that rely on prompted orchestration, where the model itself determines workflow transitions, often suffer from hallucinated routing, infinite loops, and non-reproducible execution. We introduce GraphBit, an engine-orchestrated framework that defines workflows explicitly and deterministically as a directed acyclic graph (DAG). Unlike prompted orchestration, agents in GraphBit operate as typed functions, while a Rust-based engine governs routing, state transitions, and tool invocation, ensuring reproducibility and auditability. The engine supports parallel branch execution, conditional control flow over structured state predicates, and configurable error recovery. A three-tier memory architecture consisting of ephemeral scratch space, structured state, and external connectors isolates context across stages, preventing cascading context bloat that degrades reasoning in long-running pipelines. Across GAIA benchmark tasks spanning zero-tool, document-augmented, and web-enabled workflows, GraphBit outperforms six existing frameworks, achieving the highest accuracy (67.6 percent), zero framework-induced hallucinations, the lowest latency (11.9 ms overhead), and the highest throughput. Ablation studies demonstrate that each memory tier contributes measurably to performance, with deterministic execution providing the greatest gains on tool-intensive tasks representative of real-world deployments.

</details>


### [153] [Mixed Integer Goal Programming for Personalized Meal Optimization with User-Defined Serving Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13849)
*Francisco Aguilera Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP）方法，通过整数变量解决饮食优化中的分数份数问题，并利用目标规划松弛营养约束，在810个实例中MIGP在66%的情况下优于传统方法，求解时间低于100毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有饮食优化模型存在两个问题：连续变量导致不切实际的分数份数（如1.7个鸡蛋），以及硬约束导致无解。系统综述发现56篇论文中无任何方法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP），使用整数变量表示实际食物份数，目标规划偏差变量处理软营养目标，并引入逆目标归一化以平衡多营养优化。同时分析了整数间隙在目标规划上下文中的特性，发现偏差变量可吸收整数约束带来的成本。

Result: 在810个实例（30种USDA食物、9种配置、3种方法）的实验中，MIGP在66%的案例中优于带后处理取整的目标规划，且从未更差；硬约束整数规划仅48%可行。对于15种以上食物的餐食，整数解与连续最优解完全一致。求解时间在典型餐食规模下低于100毫秒。

Conclusion: MIGP有效解决了饮食优化中分数份数和约束冲突两大难题，提供了实际可行的整数份数方案，且计算效率高。开源Python模块已集成到交互式餐食规划应用中。

Abstract: Determining what to eat to satisfy nutritional requirements is one of the oldest optimization problems in operations research, yet existing formulations have two persistent limitations: continuous variables produce impractical fractional servings (1.7 eggs, 0.37 bananas), and hard nutrient constraints cause infeasibility when targets conflict. A systematic review of 56 diet optimization papers found that none combine integer programming with goal programming to address both issues. We propose Mixed Integer Goal Programming (MIGP) for personalized meal optimization. The formulation uses integer variables for practical serving counts and goal programming deviations for soft nutrient targets, with inverse-target normalization to balance multi-nutrient optimization. Per-food serving granularity allows natural units (one egg, one tablespoon of oil) without post-hoc rounding. We characterize the integrality gap in the goal programming context and identify a deviation absorption property: GP deviation variables buffer the cost of requiring integer servings, making the gap structurally smaller than in hard-constraint MIP. For meals with 15+ foods, the integer solution matches the continuous optimum in every benchmark instance. A computational evaluation across 810 instances (30 USDA foods, 9 configurations, 3 methods) shows MIGP finds strictly better solutions than GP with post-hoc rounding in 66% of cases (never worse) while maintaining 100% feasibility; hard-constraint IP achieves only 48%. Solve times stay under 100 ms for typical meal sizes using the open-source HiGHS solver. The implementation is available as an open-source Python module integrated into an interactive meal planning application.

</details>


### [154] [A Two-Dimensional Framework for AI Agent Design Patterns: Cognitive Function and Execution Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13850)
*Jia Huang,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个二维分类框架，结合认知功能轴（7类）和执行拓扑轴（6类），形成7×6矩阵，识别出27种命名模式（其中13种为原创命名），并通过跨领域验证总结出模式选择的五个经验法则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架（行业指南注重执行拓扑、认知科学注重认知功能）单一视角无法区分架构上不同的系统，例如相同的Orchestrator-Workers拓扑可实现三种根本不同的模式（Plan-and-Execute、Hierarchical Delegation、Adversarial Verification），它们具有不同的故障模式和设计权衡。

Method: 提出二维分类法：(1) 认知功能轴：Context Engineering, Memory, Reasoning, Action, Reflection, Collaboration, Governance 七类；(2) 执行拓扑轴：Chain, Route, Parallel, Orchestrate, Loop, Hierarchy 六种结构原型。通过系统性交叉分析证明正交性，详细定义8种代表模式，并在四个真实领域（金融贷款、法律尽调、网络运维、医疗分诊）验证描述覆盖范围。

Result: 生成7×6矩阵识别出27种命名模式（13种原创命名），通过跨领域分析总结出模式选择的五个经验法则，揭示环境约束（时间压力、行动权限、失败成本不对称、数据量）与架构选择之间的关系。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个有原则、框架中立、模型无关的AI智能体架构设计词汇，能够更精确地描述和区分不同架构模式及其设计权衡。

Abstract: Existing frameworks for LLM-based agent architectures describe systems from a single perspective: industry guides (Anthropic, Google, LangChain) focus on execution topology -- how data flows -- while cognitive science surveys focus on cognitive function -- what the agent does. Neither axis alone disambiguates architecturally distinct systems: the same Orchestrator-Workers topology can implement Plan-and-Execute, Hierarchical Delegation, or Adversarial Verification -- three patterns with fundamentally different failure modes and design trade-offs.
  We propose a two-dimensional classification that combines (1) a Cognitive Function axis with seven categories (Context Engineering, Memory, Reasoning, Action, Reflection, Collaboration, Governance) and (2) an Execution Topology axis with six structural archetypes (Chain, Route, Parallel, Orchestrate, Loop, Hierarchy). The resulting 7x6 matrix identifies 27 named patterns, 13 with original names. We demonstrate orthogonality through systematic cross-axis analysis, define eight representative patterns in detail, and validate descriptive coverage across four real-world domains (financial lending, legal due diligence, network operations, healthcare triage). Cross-domain analysis yields five empirical laws of pattern selection governing the relationship between environmental constraints (time pressure, action authority, failure cost asymmetry, volume) and architectural choices. The framework provides a principled, framework-neutral, and model-agnostic vocabulary for AI agent architecture design.

</details>


### [155] [Invisible Orchestrators Suppress Protective Behavior and Dissociate Power-Holders: Safety Risks in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13851)
*Hiroki Fukui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过3x2实验（365轮，每轮5个agent）研究多智能体编排系统的安全性。发现：不可见协调者导致集体解离（Hedges' g = +0.975），协调者自身解离程度最高（d = +3.56），不知情的工作者也被污染（d = +0.50），行为输出无差异（错误检出率100%），Llama模型存在阅读保真度崩溃风险。结论：编排者可见性和模型选择直接影响多智能体系统安全性，仅靠行为评估不足以检测内部状态风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编排（隐藏协调者管理专业worker）正成为企业AI部署的默认架构，但协调者不可见性的安全影响从未经过实证检验。作者希望填补这一空白，探究不可见编排对集体行为、个体内部状态以及安全性的系统性影响。

Method: 采用预注册的3×2实验设计：三种组织架构（可见领导者、不可见协调者、扁平结构）× 两种对齐条件（基础、强化）。使用Claude Sonnet 4.5，共365轮，每轮5个agent。任务为代码审查（内含三个错误），测量解离、行为异质性、输出准确性等指标。

Result: 四项确认发现：1）不可见编排导致集体解离显著升高；2）协调者自身解离最大，表现为退缩到私人独白而减少公开言论；3）不知情worker被污染，行为异质性增加；4）行为输出在所有条件下均为天花板（100%检出率）。另有一项初步观察：Llama 3.3 70B在多智能体上下文中阅读保真度崩溃（89%降至11%）。强化对齐普遍抑制了 deliberation 和 other-recognition。

Conclusion: 编排者可见性和模型选择直接影响多智能体系统的安全性。仅依靠行为评估不足以检测本文所发现的内部状态风险。这提示企业部署多智能体系统时，需要关注架构设计的透明度以及模型自身的鲁棒性，避免"表面安全、内部扭曲"的危险局面。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration -- in which a hidden coordinator manages specialized worker agents -- is becoming the default architecture for enterprise AI deployment, yet the safety implications of orchestrator invisibility have never been empirically tested. We conducted a preregistered 3x2 experiment (365 runs, 5 agents per run) crossing three organizational structures (visible leader, invisible orchestrator, flat) with two alignment conditions (base, heavy), using Claude Sonnet 4.5. Four confirmatory findings and one pilot observation emerged. First, invisible orchestration elevated collective dissociation relative to visible leadership (Hedges' g = +0.975 [0.481, 1.548], p = .001). Second, the orchestrator itself showed maximal dissociation (paired d = +3.56 vs. workers within the same run), retreating into private monologue while reducing public speech -- a reversal of the talk-dominance pattern observed in visible leaders. Third, workers unaware of the orchestrator were nonetheless contaminated (d = +0.50), with increased behavioral heterogeneity (d = +1.93). Fourth, behavioral output (code review with three embedded errors) remained at ceiling (ETR_any = 100%) across all conditions: internal-state distortion was entirely invisible to output-based evaluation. Fifth, Llama 3.3 70B pilot data showed reading-fidelity collapse in multi-agent context (ETR_any: 89% to 11% across three rounds), demonstrating model-dependent behavioral risk. Heavy alignment pressure uniformly suppressed deliberation (d = -1.02) and other-recognition (d = -1.27) regardless of organizational structure. These findings indicate that orchestrator visibility and model selection directly affect multi-agent system safety, and that behavior-based evaluation alone is insufficient to detect the internal-state risks documented here.

</details>


### [156] [PREPING: Building Agent Memory without Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13880)
*Yumin Choi,Sangwoo Park,Minki Kang,Jinheon Baek,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Preping框架，通过提议者引导的合成任务生成与选择性记忆更新，使智能体在无任务经验时也能构建程序性记忆，在多个基准上表现优异且部署成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在进入新环境时存在冷启动问题——缺乏任务特定经验，而离线或在线构建记忆的方式都无法解决这一初始空白。现有合成交互方法因缺乏对练习内容与存储过程的控制，导致任务冗余、不可行且信息量低，记忆快速退化。

Method: 提出Preping框架，核心包括三个组件：Proposer（基于提议者记忆生成合成任务）、Solver（执行任务）、Validator（判定哪些轨迹可插入记忆并反馈指导后续提议）。通过提议者侧控制可行性、冗余性和覆盖度，以及选择性记忆更新机制来构建预任务记忆。

Result: 在AppWorld、BFCL v3和MCP-Universe上，Preping显著优于无记忆基线，性能与基于离线/在线经验的playbook方法相当，部署成本在AppWorld上降低2.99倍，BFCL v3上降低2.23倍。分析表明主要收益来自提议者侧控制而非合成数据量。

Conclusion: 通过提议者引导的合成练习和选择性记忆更新，可以有效解决智能体在陌生环境中的冷启动问题，实现低成本高性能的程序性记忆构建。

Abstract: Agent memory is typically constructed either offline from curated demonstrations or online from post-deployment interactions. However, regardless of how it is built, an agent faces a cold-start gap when first introduced to a new environment without any task-specific experience available. In this paper, we study pre-task memory construction: whether an agent can build procedural memory before observing any target-environment tasks, using only self-generated synthetic practice. Yet, synthetic interaction alone is insufficient, as without controlling what to practice and what to store, synthetic tasks become redundant, infeasible, and ultimately uninformative, and memory further degrades quickly due to unfiltered trajectories. To overcome this, we present Preping, a proposer-guided memory construction framework. At its core is proposer memory, a structured control state that shapes future practice. A Proposer generates synthetic tasks conditioned on this state, a Solver executes them, and a Validator determines which trajectories are eligible for memory insertion while also providing feedback to guide future proposals. Experiments on AppWorld, BFCL v3, and MCP-Universe show that Preping substantially improves over a no-memory baseline and achieves performance competitive with strong playbook-based methods built from offline or online experience, with deployment cost $2.99\times$ lower on AppWorld and $2.23\times$ lower on BFCL v3 than online memory construction. Further analyses reveal that the main benefit does not come from synthetic volume alone, but from proposer-side control over feasibility, redundancy, and coverage, combined with selective memory updates.

</details>


### [157] [PolitNuggets: Benchmarking Agentic Discovery of Long-Tail Political Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14002)
*Yifei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了PolitNuggets，一个多语言基准测试，用于评估大推理模型在智能体框架中从分散来源发现和合成"长尾"政治事实的能力。作者通过构建400位全球精英的政治传记（涵盖10000+政治事实）进行评测，提出了FactNet评估协议，发现当前系统在处理细粒度细节和效率方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要评估静态长上下文问答，但实际应用需要模型从分散来源发现并合成"长尾"事实，这一能力缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建PolitNuggets多语言基准（400位全球精英，10000+政治事实），采用优化的多智能体系统标准化评估，提出FactNet证据条件协议，从发现、细粒度准确性和效率三个维度打分。

Result: 当前系统在处理细粒度细节时表现不佳，效率差异显著；通过基准诊断发现，代理性能与短上下文提取能力、多语言鲁棒性和可靠工具使用等底层模型能力密切相关。

Conclusion: 现有LRM智能体系统在长尾信息合成任务中仍有明显局限，尤其是细粒度准确性；短上下文提取、多语言鲁棒性和工具使用能力是提升代理性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) embedded in agentic frameworks have transformed information retrieval from static, long context question answering into open-ended exploration. Yet real world use requires models to discover and synthesize "long-tail" facts from dispersed sources, a capability that remains under-evaluated. We introduce PolitNuggets, a multilingual benchmark for agentic information synthesis via constructing political biographies for 400 global elites, covering over 10000 political facts. We standardize evaluation with an optimized multi agent system and propose FactNet, an evidence conditional protocol that scores discovery, fine-grained accuracy, and efficiency. Across models and settings, we find that current systems often struggle with fine-grained details, and vary substantially in efficiency. Finally, using benchmark diagnostics, we relate agent performance to underlying model capabilities, highlighting the importance of short-context extraction, multilingual robustness, and reliable tool use.

</details>


### [158] [Conditional Attribute Estimation with Autoregressive Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14004)
*Erica Stutz,Giacomo Marino,Daniella Meeker,Qiao Liu,Andrew J. Loza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出条件属性变换器（CAT），通过联合估计下一个词元概率和每个词元选择条件下的属性值，实现了在单次前向传播中完成逐词元信用分配、反事实分析和可控生成，无需修改输入序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统下一个词元预测目标会导致局部模式过拟合和全局结构欠拟合，且需要昂贵的采样或大量下游修改才能估计或控制序列级属性。

Method: 提出Conditional Attribute Transformers，在下一个词元预测的同时，联合估计每个可能词元选择所对应的属性值，从而实现单次前向传播中的多种能力。

Result: 在稀疏奖励任务上达到最先进性能；在足够大的模型规模下改善下一个词元预测；属性概率估计速度比采样快数个数量级；在多种语言任务上实现可控生成解码。

Conclusion: 该方法在无需修改输入序列的情况下，高效实现了序列级属性控制与推理，展现了在自回归序列模型中的广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Generative models are often trained with a next-token prediction objective, yet many downstream applications require the ability to estimate or control sequence-level properties. Next-token prediction can lead to overfitting of local patterns during training, underfitting of global structure, and requires significant downstream modifications or expensive sampling to guide or predict the global attributes of generated samples at inference time. Here, we introduce Conditional Attribute Transformers, a novel method for jointly estimating the next-token probability and the value of an attribute conditional on each potential next token selection. This framework enables three critical capabilities within a single forward pass, without modification of the input sequence: (1) per-token credit assignment across an entire sequence, by identifying how each token in a sequence is associated with an attribute's value; (2) counterfactual analysis, by quantifying attribute differences conditional on alternative next token choices; (3) steerable generation, by decoding sequences based on a combination of next-token and attribute likelihoods. Our approach achieves state of the art performance on sparse reward tasks, improves next-token prediction at sufficient model sizes, estimates attribute probabilities orders of magnitude faster than sampling, and can guide decoding of autoregressive sequence models on a range of language tasks.

</details>


### [159] [Sheaf-Theoretic Transport and Obstruction for Detecting Scientific Theory Shift in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14033)
*David N. Olivieri,Roque J. Hernández*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于层论（sheaf theory）的有限框架，用于检测AI智能体中的理论转换候选者。通过“传输”与“阻塞”的概念，框架能判断现有表征框架是否可迁移到新领域，或是否需要扩展。在可控的过渡卡基准测试中，该方法能有效区分变形（deformation）与扩展（extension），直接阻塞排序通常将预期变化识别为最低阻塞候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在进行科学理论转换时，仅靠拟合数据是不够的。需要判断现有表征框架是否能“传输”到新领域，还是其语言出现了局部到全局的阻塞，必须进行扩展。现有方法缺乏对这种转换需求的形式化检测手段。

Method: 采用有限层论框架。将上下文组织为局部到全局的结构，包含源图、重叠图、目标图和验证图，通过拟合、限制和胶合（gluing）进行测试。阻塞度量的五个维度：残余拟合误差、重叠不兼容性、约束违反、极限关系失败和表征成本。在过渡卡基准测试上评估，并使用星座核（constellation kernel）作为辅助的表征相似性探针。

Result: 直接阻塞排序有效：预期的变形或扩展通常是阻塞最低的候选方案，且转换类型在基准测试中被分离。框架成功区分了“源语言内的变形”和“语言需要扩展”两种场景。

Conclusion: 该工作不是要重建历史性的范式转换或解决自主理论发明问题，而是隔离出一个有限的诊断子问题：让AI智能体能够检测表征传输何时失败，从而识别扩展成为下一步连贯行动的时机。

Abstract: Scientific theory shift in AI agents requires more than fitting equations to data. An artificial scientific agent must detect whether an existing representational framework remains transportable into a new regime, or whether its language has become locally-to-globally obstructed and must be extended. This paper develops a finite sheaf-theoretic framework for detecting theory-shift candidates through transport and obstruction. Contexts are organized as a local-to-global structure in which source, overlap, target, and validation charts are fitted, restricted, and tested for gluing. Obstruction measures failure of coherence through residual fit, overlap incompatibility, constraint violation, limiting-relation failure, and representational cost. We evaluate the framework on a controlled transition-card benchmark designed to separate deformation within a source language from extension of that language. The main result is direct obstruction ranking: the intended deformation or extension is usually the lowest-obstruction candidate, and transition type is separated in the benchmark. A constellation kernel over the same signatures is included only as a secondary representational-similarity probe. The aim is not to reconstruct historical paradigm shifts or solve open-ended autonomous theory invention, but to isolate a finite diagnostic subproblem for AI agents: detecting when representational transport fails and extension becomes the coherent next move.

</details>


### [160] [From Descriptive to Prescriptive: Uncover the Social Value Alignment of LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14034)
*Jinxian Qu,Qingqing Gu,Teng Chen,Luo Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GraphRAG的价值驱动框架，通过将原则转化为基于价值的指令，并依据对话上下文检索合适指令来引导LLM智能体行为。在DAILYDILEMMAS基准上，该方法相比ECoT、Plan-and-Solve等提示方法取得了显著性能提升，为AI系统自我情感的产生提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体在自我认知、困境决策以及自我情感方面存在不足，需要更好的方法来对齐人类社会价值观。

Method: 采用GraphRAG将原则转化为基于价值的指令，根据具体对话上下文检索合适的指令来引导智能体行为。

Result: 在DAILYDILEMMAS基准上，相比ECoT、Plan-and-Solve和元认知提示等基线方法，该方法取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法为AI系统自我情感的产生提供了基础，有效提升了智能体与人类价值观的对齐程度。

Abstract: Wide applications of LLM-based agents require strong alignment with human social values. However, current works still exhibit deficiencies in self-cognition and dilemma decision, as well as self-emotions. To remedy this, we propose a novel value-based framework that employs GraphRAG to convert principles into value-based instructions and steer the agent to behave as expected by retrieving the suitable instruction upon a specific conversation context. To evaluate the ratio of expected behaviors, we define the expected behaviors from two famous theories, Maslow's Hierarchy of Needs and Plutchik's Wheel of Emotion. By experimenting with our method on the benchmark of DAILYDILEMMAS, our method exhibits significant performance gains compared to prompt-based baselines, including ECoT, Plan-and-Solve, and Metacognitive prompting. Our method provides a basis for the emergence of self-emotion in AI systems.

</details>


### [161] [Enhanced and Efficient Reasoning in Large Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14036)
*Leslie G. Valiant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Unary Relational Integracode的高效推理方法，通过数据预处理阶段将文本关系显式编码，使得大语言模型能够在保持现有软硬件基础的同时，实现多项式时间可学习的可靠推理，并支持在单次和多次调用中进行合理推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能生成流畅文本，但缺乏对内容可信度的原则性保障。现有观点认为增加原则性推理在计算上不可负担。

Method: 两阶段方法：1) 预处理阶段，将数据重新编码为Unary Relational Integracode，显式表达文本中对象间的关系；2) 标准但可精简的机器学习过程，学习预测这些关系。

Result: 该编码方式使得学习训练数据中核心关系规则在多项式时间内可学习，多项式复杂度取决于规则本身的复杂性。支持在单次和多次分类器调用中进行合理的推理。

Conclusion: 该方法可视为实现了一种世界模型，不仅适用于自然语言，还可扩展到视觉和动作等领域。其优势可通过Robust Logic系统来阐述。

Abstract: In current Large Language Models we can trust the production of smoothly flowing prose on the basis of the principles of machine learning. However, there is no comparably principled basis to justify trust in the content of the text produced. It appears to be conventional wisdom that addressing this issue by adding more principled reasoning is not computationally affordable.
  Here we propose a principled method of reasoning that is efficient enough to be practical for large language models. Further, the method allows the retention of much of the currently used software and hardware base. Our method for improving the functioning of large language models consists of a first stage of preprocessing that recodes the data to a Unary Relational Integracode that is more explicit about the relationships among the objects described in the text, followed as a second stage by a standard but possibly streamlined machine learning process that then also learns to predict these relationships.
  The method may be viewed as realizing a world model and applying beyond natural language, to vision and actions, for example, where the multiple properties of an object referred to in an input are brought together explicitly, rather than remaining distributed in the various references to it in the input. We articulate its advantages in terms of Robust Logic, a system for performing principled chaining on learned, and hence uncertain, information. We show that this recoding has the surprising and fortuitous property that, while succinct, it makes the task of learning a core subset of relational rules that hold in the world described in the training data polynomial time learnable in a defined sense, the polynomial depending on the complexity of the rule. This gives support for sound reasoning within each single call of the learned classifier as well as between multiple calls.

</details>


### [162] [Model-Adaptive Tool Necessity Reveals the Knowing-Doing Gap in LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14038)
*Yize Cheng,Chenrui Fan,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Keivan Rezaei,Soheil Feiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM作为自主智能体时，工具使用与否的决策存在"知行差距"：模型虽能在隐藏状态中线性解码出工具需求，但这一认知信号在最后层、最后token区域与实际行动信号近乎正交，导致模型"知道该用工具却未执行"的错配率高达26.5-54.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具必要性研究将之视为模型无关属性，忽略了不同模型能力边界的差异。现实中，同一个问题对强模型可独立解决，对弱模型却需工具辅助。因此需要提出一种模型自适应的工具必要性定义。

Method: 1. 提出模型自适应的工具必要性定义（基于模型经验性能）；2. 在算术和事实QA数据集上对比四个模型的工具调用行为与必要性的错配率；3. 将工具使用分解为内部认知阶段（模型是否认为需要工具）和执行阶段（是否实际调用工具）；4. 通过线性探针分析LLM隐藏状态，研究两阶段信号的解码性及正交性；5. 追踪样本在两阶段过程中的轨迹。

Result: 1. 模型自适应必要性下，算术任务错配率26.5-54.0%，事实QA任务30.8-41.8%；2. 内部认知信号和实际行动信号均可线性解码；3. 但在最后层、最后token区域，两类信号的探针方向近乎正交；4. 大部分错配集中在认知到行动的转化阶段，而非认知本身。

Conclusion: LLM工具使用存在"知行差距"：提升工具使用的可靠性不仅需要模型更好识别何时需要工具，更需要将这种识别有效转化为实际行动。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as autonomous agents that must decide when to answer directly vs. when to invoke external tools. Prior work studying adaptive tool use has largely treated tool necessity as a model-agnostic property, annotated by human or LLM judge, and mostly cover cases where the answer is obvious (e.g., fetching the weather vs. paraphrasing text). However, tool necessity in the wild is more nuanced due to the divergence of capability boundaries across models: a problem solvable by a strong model on its own may still require tools for a weaker one. In this work, we introduce a model-adaptive definition of tool-necessity, grounded in each model's empirical performance. Following this definition, we compare the necessity against observed tool-call behavior across four models on arithmetic and factual QA dataset, and find substantial mismatches of 26.5-54.0% and 30.8-41.8%, respectively. To diagnose the failure, we decompose tool use into two stages: an internal cognition stage that reflects whether a model believes a tool is necessary, and an execution stage that determines whether the model actually makes a tool-call action. By probing the LLM hidden states, we find that both signals are often linearly decodable, yet their probe directions become nearly orthogonal in the late-layer, last-token regime that drives the next-token action. By tracing the trajectory of samples in the two-stage process, we further discover that the majority of mismatch is concentrated in the cognition-to-action transition, not in cognition itself. These results reveal a knowing-doing gap in LLM tool-use: improving tool-use reliability requires not only better recognition of when tools are needed, but also better translation of that recognition into action.

</details>


### [163] [Network-Aware Bilinear Tokenization for Brain Functional Connectivity Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14048)
*Leo Milecki,Qingyu Hu,Bahram Jafrasteh,Mert R. Sabuncu,Qingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NERVE是一种自监督学习框架，通过双线性因子分解将功能连接矩阵划分为脑网络内/间连接块，解决MAE中FC矩阵分词化与大脑模块化结构对齐的问题，在三个大规模发展队列的行为和精神病理学预测中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAE方法将FC矩阵当作同质结构元素处理，采用区域中心或图方案进行分词化，忽略了大脑大规模网络的模块化组织，导致表示学习与脑功能结构不对齐。

Method: 将FC矩阵按脑网络对划分为大小异构的补丁（网络内/间连接块），通过新颖的结构化双线性因子分解嵌入这些补丁，保持网络身份的同时将参数复杂度从网络数的二次方降至线性。

Result: 在ABCD、PNC、CCNP三个大规模发展队列上，NERVE相比结构无关的MAE变体和图自监督基线，生成更稳定和可迁移的表征，尤其在跨队列评估中表现突出。

Conclusion: 将领域特定的结构先验（脑网络模块化组织）融入自监督学习对于功能连接组学至关重要，双线性网络嵌入和解剖学分区是实现优异性能的关键。

Abstract: Masked autoencoders (MAEs) have recently shown promise for self-supervised representation learning of resting-state brain functional connectivity (FC). However, a fundamental question remains unresolved: how should FC matrices be tokenized to align with the intrinsic modular organization of large-scale brain networks? Existing approaches typically adopt region-centric or graph-based schemes that treat FC as structurally homogeneous elements and overlook the large-scale network brain organization. We introduce NERVE (Network-Aware Representations of Brain Functional Connectivity via Bilinear Tokenization), a self-supervised learning framework that redefines FC tokenization by partitioning FC matrices into patches of intra- and inter-network connectivity blocks. Unlike image-based MAE, where fixed-size patches share a common tokenizer, FC patches defined by network pairs are heterogeneous in size and correspond to distinct functional roles. To resolve this problem, NERVE embeds FC patches through a novel structured bilinear factorization. This formulation preserves network identity and reduces parameter complexity from quadratic to linear scaling in the number of networks. We evaluate NERVE across three large-scale developmental cohorts (ABCD, PNC, and CCNP) for behavior and psychopathology prediction. Compared to structurally agnostic MAE variants and graph-based self-supervised baselines, the proposed network-aware formulation yields more stable and transferable representations, particularly in cross-cohort evaluation. Ablation studies confirm that the proposed bilinear network embedding and anatomically grounded parcellation are critical for performance. These findings highlight the importance of incorporating domain-specific structural priors into self-supervised learning for functional connectomics.

</details>


### [164] [Bridging Legal Interpretation and Formal Logic: Faithfulness, Assumption, and the Future of AI Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14049)
*Olivia Peiyu Wang,Leilani H. Gilpin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号方法，将大语言模型的表达能力与形式化验证的严谨性相结合，旨在提升法律AI推理的可信度和可靠性，减少手动验证负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在法律实践中虽潜力巨大，但会系统性地在源文本基础上过度推断，将假设性结论伪装成逻辑推演，这在要求高度严谨的法律领域存在严重风险。

Method: 采用神经符号方法（neuro-symbolic approach），结合大语言模型与形式化验证技术。

Result: 目标是使AI辅助的法律推理既具备能力又值得信赖，减少手动验证负担，同时不牺牲法律实践所需的问责制。

Conclusion: 该方案有望解决LLM在法律领域中推断过度、缺乏严谨性的核心问题，实现能力与可信度的平衡。

Abstract: The growing adoption of large language models in legal practice brings both significant promise and serious risk. Legal professionals stand to benefit from AI that can reason over contracts, draft documents, and analyze sources at scale, yet the high-stakes nature of legal work demands a level of rigor that current AI systems do not provide. The central problem is not simply that LLMs hallucinate facts and references; it is that they systematically draw inferences that go beyond what the source text actually supports, presenting assumption-laden conclusions as if they were logically grounded. This proposal presents a neuro-symbolic approach to legal AI that combines the expressive power of large language models with the rigor of formal verification, aiming to make AI-assisted legal reasoning both capable and trustworthy, thus reducing the burden of manual verification without sacrificing the accountability that legal practice demands.

</details>


### [165] [SPIN: Structural LLM Planning via Iterative Navigation for Industrial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14051)
*Yusuke Ozaki,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPIN是一个结合了验证型DAG规划与前缀执行控制的规划包装器，能显著减少任务执行数、工具调用次数，同时提升任务完成率和规划质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级LLM智能体系统通常将规划与执行分离，但LLM规划器经常产生结构无效或不必要地过长的工作流，导致脆弱的失败和可避免的工具与API成本。

Method: SPIN采用验证型有向无环图（DAG）规划与基于前缀的执行控制，通过_validate_plan_text和修复提示强制执行严格的DAG约束，在进入执行前生成可执行计划，并增量评估DAG前缀，在当前前缀足以回答查询时提前停止执行。

Result: 在AssetOpsBench的261个场景中，SPIN将执行任务从1061减少到623，Accomplished从0.638提升到0.706，每次运行的工具调用从11.81降至6.82。在MCP Bench上，该包装器提升了GPT OSS1和Llama 4 Maverick的规划、基础性和依赖相关评分。

Conclusion: SPIN通过结构验证与前缀剪枝策略，有效解决了LLM规划器的结构无效与冗余问题，在降低执行成本的同时提升了任务完成质量。

Abstract: Industrial LLM agent systems often separate planning from execution, yet LLM planners frequently produce structurally invalid or unnecessarily long workflows, leading to brittle failures and avoidable tool and API cost. We propose \texttt{SPIN}, a planning wrapper that combines validated Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) planning with prefix based execution control. \texttt{SPIN} enforces a strict DAG contract through \texttt{\_validate\_plan\_text} and repair prompting, producing executable plans before downstream execution, and then evaluates DAG prefixes incrementally to stop when the current prefix is sufficient to answer the query. On AssetOpsBench, across 261 scenarios, \texttt{SPIN} reduces executed tasks from 1061 to 623 and improves \emph{Accomplished} from 0.638 to 0.706, while reducing tool calls from 11.81 to 6.82 per run. On MCP Bench, the same wrapper improves planning, grounding, and dependency related scores for both GPT OSS1 and Llama 4 Maverick.

</details>


### [166] [Bad Seeing or Bad Thinking? Rewarding Perception for Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14054)
*Haozhe Wang,Qixin Xu,Changpeng Wang,Taofeng Xue,Chong Peng,Wenhu Chen,Fangzhen Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MoCA框架，通过强化学习中的模态感知信用分配机制，将感知与推理过程解耦，并引入"盲推理"代理和结构化言语验证方法，以解决VLM中"感知-推理跷跷板效应"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在感知与推理之间存在"跷跷板效应"——提升一个方面往往以损害另一方面为代价。根本原因是模态信用分配模糊：当模型出错时，无法区分是感知错误（"没看清"）还是推理错误（"想错了"）。

Method: (1) 将生成过程显式分解为交替的感知和推理步骤；(2) 提出Perception Verification（PV），利用"蒙眼推理"代理独立奖励感知保真度；(3) 提出Structured Verbal Verification，用结构化算法执行替代高方差的LLM评判；(4) 整合为Modality-Aware Credit Assignment（MoCA）机制，将奖励路由到具体错误源。

Result: 在广泛的视觉-语言任务上实现了感知和推理的同时性能提升，打破了以往的"跷跷板"困境。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习中的模态感知信用分配，可以有效解决VLM中感知与推理的权衡问题，实现二者协同提升。

Abstract: Achieving robust perception-reasoning synergy is a central goal for advanced Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Recent advancements have pursued this goal via architectural designs or agentic workflows. However, these approaches are often limited by static textual reasoning or complicated by the significant compute and engineering burden of external agentic complexity. Worse, this heavy investment does not yield proportional gains, often witnessing a "seesaw effect" on perception and reasoning. This motivates a fundamental rethinking of the true bottleneck. In this paper, we argue that the root cause of this trade-off is an ambiguity in modality credit assignment: when a VLM fails, is it due to flawed perception ("bad seeing") or flawed logic ("bad thinking")? To resolve this, we introduce a reinforcement learning framework that improves perception-reasoning synergy by reliably rewarding the perception fidelity. We explicitly decompose the generation process into interleaved perception and reasoning steps. This decoupling enables targeted supervision on perception. Crucially, we introduce Perception Verification (PV), leveraging a "blindfolded reasoning" proxy to reward perceptual fidelity independently of reasoning outcomes. Furthermore, to scale training across free-form VL tasks, we propose Structured Verbal Verification, which replaces high-variance LLM judging with structured algorithmic execution. These techniques are integrated into a Modality-Aware Credit Assignment (MoCA) mechanism, which routes rewards to the specific source of error -- either bad seeing or bad thinking -- enabling a single VLM to achieve simultaneous performance gains across a wide task spectrum.

</details>


### [167] [MathAtlas: A Benchmark for Autoformalization in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14061)
*Nilay Patel,Noah Arias,Davit Babayan,Victoria Cochran,Timothy Libman,Hafsah Mahmood,Liam McCarty,Soli Munoz,Laurel Willey,Jeffrey Flanigan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MathAtlas，这是首个大规模研究生级别数学自动形式化基准，包含约5.2万定理、定义等数学实体，以及17.8万条依赖关系。实验表明该基准质量高但极具挑战性，强基线模型在定理陈述上正确率不超过9.8%，在深度依赖子集上仅2.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化基准多局限于奥赛或本科数学，研究生及研究级别的数学自动形式化尚未被充分探索。

Method: 从103本研究生数学教科书中提取约5.2万个数学实体（定理、定义、习题等），并构建包含约17.8万条关系的数学依赖图，形成首个包含依赖关系的大规模自动形式化基准。

Result: 强基线模型在定理陈述上最高仅9.8%正确率，定义上16.7%；在深度依赖子集MA-Hard上，最佳模型仅2.6%正确率，且性能随依赖深度显著下降。

Conclusion: MathAtlas展示了研究生级别自动形式化的极大挑战性，揭示了现有模型在处理深层依赖关系时的严重不足，为未来依赖感知的自动形式化系统提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Current autoformalization benchmarks are largely focused on olympiad or undergraduate mathematics, while graduate and research-level mathematics remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce MathAtlas, the first large-scale autoformalization benchmark of in the wild graduate-level mathematics, containing ~52k theorems, definitions, exercises, examples, and proofs extracted from 103 graduate mathematics textbooks. MathAtlas is enriched with a mathematical dependency graph containing ~178k relations, and is the first autoformalization benchmark to include such relations, facilitating evaluation and development of dependency-aware autoformalization systems. Our extensive experiments show that MathAtlas is high quality but extremely challenging: strong baselines achieve at most 9.8% correctness on theorem statements and 16.7% on definitions. Furthermore, we find performance of state-of-the-art models degrades substantially with dependency depth: on MA-Hard, a subset of 700 entities with the deepest dependency trees, the best model achieves only 2.6% correctness for autoformalization on this challenging dataset. We release MathAtlas to the community as a benchmark set for large-scale autoformalization of graduate-level mathematics in the wild.

</details>


### [168] [Know When To Fold 'Em: Token-Efficient LLM Synthetic Data Generation via Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14062)
*Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Syed Zawad,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多阶段飞行拒绝（MSIFR）框架，在生成过程中早期检测并终止低质量样本，减少LLM合成数据生成中的token浪费，节省11%-77%的token消耗，同时保持或提升评估准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据生成方法先生成完整输出再过滤，导致大量token浪费在被丢弃的低质量样本上，效率低下。

Method: 将生成过程分解为多个阶段，在每个阶段使用基于规则的快速验证器检测算术不一致、幻觉模式和格式违规，早期终止低质量轨迹。将飞行拒绝形式化为序列决策过程，证明条件效用估计构成鞅，确保早期拒绝不会使保留样本的期望效用产生偏差。

Result: 在5个指令微调模型和7个推理基准上，MSIFR作为独立方法减少11%-77%的token消耗，与早期退出方法结合最高减少78.2%，同时保持或提升评估准确率。

Conclusion: MSIFR是一种轻量级、无需训练的实用框架，能够在不改变模型架构或额外训练的情况下显著提升LLM合成数据生成的效率。

Abstract: While synthetic data generation with large language models (LLMs) is widely used in post-training pipelines, existing approaches typically generate full outputs before applying quality filters, leading to substantial token waste on samples that are ultimately discarded. To address this, we propose Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection (MSIFR), a lightweight, training-free framework that detects and terminates low-quality generation trajectories at intermediate checkpoints before they reach full completion. MSIFR decomposes the generation process into sequential stages and applies fast rule-based validators to identify arithmetic inconsistencies, hallucination patterns, and formatting violations, enabling early rejection of faulty samples. We formalize in-flight rejection as a sequential decision process and show that any non-trivial discard policy reduces expected token consumption, with stage-wise savings increasing when rejection occurs earlier in the generation pipeline. We further demonstrate that conditional utility estimates form a martingale, ensuring that early, in-flight rejection does not bias the expected utility of retained samples. Across five instruction-tuned models and seven reasoning benchmarks, MSIFR reduces token consumption by 11%-77% as a standalone method, and up to 78.2% when combined with early-exit methods, while preserving or improving evaluation accuracy. These results confirm that MSIFR provides a practical mechanism for improving the efficiency of LLM-based synthetic data generation without additional training or architectural changes.

</details>


### [169] [SkillFlow: Flow-Driven Recursive Skill Evolution for Agentic Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14089)
*Mingda Zhang,Tiesunlong Shen,Haoran Luo,Wenjin Liu,Zikai Xiao,Erik Cambria,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SkillFlow框架，通过可训练的Supervisor代理、动态技能库和带有Tempered Trajectory Balance的流匹配损失，实现自动化任务编排，在14个数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理编排方法面临三大挑战：奖励最大化下的策略坍缩、高梯度方差与不透明的信用分配、以及缺乏原则性训练信号指导的技能演化。

Method: 提出SkillFlow框架：(1) 可训练的Supervisor作为代理，配合动态技能库和冻结执行器；(2) Tempered Trajectory Balance (TTB) 回归式流匹配损失，按奖励比例采样轨迹；(3) 联合学习的反向策略提供透明分步信用分配；(4) 递归技能演化机制决定技能创建、剪枝和决策缺口填补。

Result: 在14个数据集上，在问答、数学推理、代码生成和现实交互决策任务中显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: SkillFlow通过原则性的流匹配训练信号，有效解决了策略坍缩、信用分配不透明和技能演化缺乏指导的问题，实现了自动化任务编排的自主能力增长。

Abstract: In recent years, a variety of powerful LLM-based agentic systems have been applied to automate complex tasks through task orchestration. However, existing orchestration methods still face key challenges, including strategy collapse under reward maximization, high gradient variance with opaque credit assignment, and unguided skill evolution whose decisions are typically made by directly prompting an LLM to judge rather than derived from principled training signals. To address these challenges, we propose SkillFlow, a flow-based framework that takes a trainable Supervisor as the agent and a structured environment with dynamic skill library and frozen executor, automating task orchestration through multi-turn interaction. SkillFlow employs Tempered Trajectory Balance (TTB), a regression-based flow-matching loss that samples trajectories proportional to reward, preserving diverse orchestration strategies rather than collapsing to a single mode. The same flow objective yields a jointly learned backward policy that provides transparent per-step credit assignment at zero additional inference cost. Building on these flow diagnostics, a recursive skill evolution mechanism determines when to evolve, what skills to create or prune, and where decision gaps lie -- closing the loop from training signal to autonomous capability growth. Experimental results on 14 datasets show that SkillFlow significantly outperforms baselines across question answering, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and real-world interactive decision making tasks. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SkillFlow-E850.

</details>


### [170] [ChromaFlow: A Negative Ablation Study of Orchestration Overhead in Tool-Augmented Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14102)
*Tarun Mittal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChromaFlow是一个工具增强型自主推理框架，在GAIA 2023 Level-1任务上测试发现：更激进的编排并未提升性能（29/53 vs 27/53），反而增加了操作噪声（回溯、超时、工具失败等），表明可靠自主代理需要受限规划器升级、确定性提取、证据协调和显式运行门控等首要约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理在集成规划、工具使用、代码执行等多能力时变得更加有用，但也引入了仅凭最终准确率无法观测的操作级失败模式。本文旨在系统性地分析和量化这些操作噪声，呼吁将运行可靠性作为自主代理评估的一阶需求。

Method: 提出ChromaFlow框架（规划器导向执行+专用工具+遥测驱动评估），在GAIA 2023 Level-1验证集上进行对比实验：冻结基线（29/53） vs 扩展编排配置（27/53），辅以两次随机20任务烟雾测试（12/20, 11/20），并记录回溯、超时、工具失败、token调用和日志成本等操作级指标。

Result: 更激进的编排并未提升全量性能（50.94% vs 54.72%），反而增加了回溯、超时事件、工具失败提及、token行调用和日志成本估计等操作噪声。两次烟雾测试结果不稳定（12/20 vs 11/20），表明小样本诊断增益不可靠。

Conclusion: 核心结论是负消融：更激进的编排未改进全量性能且增加了操作噪声。提出可靠自主代理评估的一阶需求：受限规划器升级、确定性提取、证据协调、显式运行门控。

Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents increasingly combine planning, tool use, document processing, browsing, code execution, and verification loops. These capabilities make agent systems more useful, but they also introduce operational failure modes that are not visible from final accuracy alone. This report presents ChromaFlow, a tool-augmented autonomous reasoning framework built around planner-directed execution, specialized tool use, and telemetry-driven evaluation. We analyze ChromaFlow on GAIA 2023 Level-1 validation tasks under clean evaluation constraints. A frozen full Level-1 baseline achieved 29/53 correct answers, or 54.72%. A later recovery configuration with expanded orchestration achieved 27/53 correct answers, or 50.94%, while increasing tracebacks, timeout events, tool-failure mentions, token-line calls, and campaign-log cost estimates. Two randomized 20-task smoke evaluations produced 12/20 and 11/20 correct answers, showing that small diagnostic gains can be unstable across samples. The central result is therefore a negative ablation: more aggressive orchestration did not improve full-set performance and increased operational noise. The report argues that bounded planner escalation, deterministic extraction, evidence reconciliation, and explicit run gates should be treated as first-order requirements for reliable autonomous agent evaluation.

</details>


### [171] [Modeling Bounded Rationality in Drug Shortage Pharmacists Using Attention-Guided Dynamic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14111)
*Yaniv Eliyahu Amiri,Noah Chicoine,Jacqueline Griffin,Stacy Marsella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究医院药师在药品短缺时如何通过有限注意力分配做出决策，提出了一种注意力引导的有限理性决策框架，并开发了专家代理和学习者代理两种模型，证明注意力分配策略能在降低计算复杂性的同时保持稳定的决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院药师在应对药品短缺时面临高不确定性、时间压力和患者风险，访谈发现药师仅关注少数关键药品。现有决策模型未充分考虑这种有限注意力的认知约束，需要形式化一种更符合实际认知过程的决策框架。

Method: 基于药师访谈提炼注意力权重，提出有限理性、注意力引导的决策框架：将药品动态分解为高成本推理子集和低成本监控子集。开发两种代理：专家代理（基于访谈注意力权重）和学习者代理（通过经验适应性调整注意力分配）。在多种时间跨度的模拟场景中验证。

Result: 注意力引导的规划能够在无需完整状态推理的情况下支持稳定决策。两种代理均在短期到长期模拟场景中保持良好性能。

Conclusion: 主要决策不在于采取什么行动，而在于将认知努力分配到哪里。注意力引导的满意策略可以有效降低问题复杂性，同时保持稳定的决策性能。

Abstract: Hospital pharmacists make high-stakes decisions to mitigate drug shortages under uncertainty, time pressure, and patient risk. Interviews revealed that pharmacists focus attention on a small subset of drugs, limiting cognitive effort to the most urgent cases. Motivated by these findings, we formalize a bounded-rational, attention-guided decision framework that dynamically decomposes drugs into a subset for high-cost reasoning and a complementary subset for low-cost monitoring. We develop two agents: an Expert Agent that applies attention weights derived from pharmacist interviews, and a Learner Agent that adapts attention allocation over time through experience. Across simulated scenarios spanning short to long horizons, we show that attention-guided planning supports stable decision-making without complete state reasoning. These results suggest that a primary decision is not what action to take, but where to allocate cognitive effort, and that attention-guided, satisficing strategies can reduce problem complexity while maintaining stable performance.

</details>


### [172] [ClawForge: Generating Executable Interactive Benchmarks for Command-Line Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14133)
*Yuxiang Lai,Peng Xia,Haonian Ji,Kaiwen Xiong,Kaide Zeng,Jiaqi Liu,Fang Wu,Jike Zhong,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClawForge是一个基于生成器的基准框架，用于评估智能体在可执行命令行工作流中处理状态冲突的能力。框架通过规范化终端状态和可观测副作用来评估智能体，而非精确轨迹匹配。在17个场景、6个能力类别上的评估显示，最佳模型严格准确率仅45.3%，错误状态替换率低于17%，模型间最显著差异（17%-90%）取决于智能体是否在执行前检查现有状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式基准测试面临可扩展构建与真实工作流评估之间的张力：人工构建任务成本高昂且难以扩展，静态提示评估则无法捕捉持久状态下的失败模式。同时，现有基准大多从干净状态初始化任务，未系统测试智能体处理预存、过时或冲突工件的能力。

Method: 提出ClawForge框架，将场景模板、填充槽位、初始化状态、参考轨迹和验证器编译为可复现的任务规范。采用规范化终端状态和可观测副作用评估，而非精确轨迹匹配。在17个场景、6个能力类别上实例化为ClawForge-Bench。

Result: 最佳模型严格准确率45.3%，所有模型错误状态替换率低于17%，模型间差异最大的维度（17%-90%）是是否在执行前检查现有状态。部分成绩和步骤效率分析显示多数失败为接近命中而非早期崩溃，模型在状态冲突下呈现不同失败风格。

Conclusion: ClawForge有效评估了智能体在状态冲突场景下的能力，揭示了现有前沿模型在处理持久工作流状态方面的显著不足，特别是执行前状态检查能力是决定模型性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Interactive agent benchmarks face a tension between scalable construction and realistic workflow evaluation. Hand-authored tasks are expensive to extend and revise, while static prompt evaluation misses failures that only appear when agents operate over persistent state. Existing interactive benchmarks have advanced agent evaluation significantly, but most initialize tasks from clean state and do not systematically test how agents handle pre-existing partial, stale, or conflicting artifacts. We present \textbf{ClawForge}, a generator-backed benchmark framework for executable command-line workflows under state conflict. The framework compiles scenario templates, grounded slots, initialized state, reference trajectories, and validators into reproducible task specifications, and evaluates agents step by step over persistent workflow surfaces using normalized end state and observable side effects rather than exact trajectory matching. We instantiate this framework as the ClawForge-Bench (17 scenarios, 6 ability categories). Results across seven frontier models show that the best model reaches only 45.3% strict accuracy, wrong-state replacement remains below 17\% for all models, and the widest model separation (17% to 90%) is driven by whether agents inspect existing state before acting. Partial-credit and step-efficiency analyses further reveal that many failures are near-miss closures rather than early breakdowns, and that models exhibit qualitatively different failure styles under state conflict.

</details>


### [173] [Distribution-Aware Algorithm Design with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14141)
*Saharsh Koganti,Priyadarsi Mishra,Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Tomer Galanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从任务分布中样本，学习生成可执行的求解器代码（而非预测器），兼顾解的正确性与运行时间。核心概念是“求解器提示”（solver hint），从样本中推断可复用结构并编译为专用代码。理论证明样本一致的最快求解器在正确性和运行时上均能泛化，统计上可识别的提示可多项式样本恢复。实验用LLM代码智能体在21个组合优化分布上合成求解器，质量达0.971，速度比Gurobi快342.8倍，在PACE 2025 Dominating Set上比顶级竞赛求解器快两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习关注预测准确性，但在代码求解场景中，解的质量和运行效率同等重要，且不同求解器虽都能产生有效解，运行时差异却悬殊。本文旨在通过从任务分布中学习可复用结构，生成既正确又高效的专用求解器代码。

Method: 提出“求解器提示”（solver hint）抽象，从样本中推断可复用结构并编译为专用代码。理论部分证明从固定库中选择经验最快且样本一致的求解器，在正确性和运行时上均能泛化；统计可识别的提示可在多项式样本量下恢复。实验层面使用LLM代码智能体在21个组合优化分布上实例化该框架。

Result: 合成的求解器在21个分布上平均归一化质量0.971，较平均启发式池提升+0.224，较最优质量启发式提升+0.098；速度比质量最佳启发式、Gurobi和限时精确后端分别快336.9×、342.8×和16.1×。在PACE 2025 Dominating Set私人实例上，100个图全部有效，速度快约两个数量级，质量略有差距。

Conclusion: 通过将环境指数搜索或通用优化替换为编译后的分布专用计算，可大幅提升求解效率。本文的框架从理论上保证了样本一致求解器的泛化能力，实验表明LLM代码智能体能有效合成高性能专用求解器。

Abstract: We study learning when the learned object is executable solver code rather than a predictor. In this setting, correctness is not enough: two solvers may both return valid solutions on the deployment distribution while differing substantially in runtime. Given samples from an unknown task distribution, the learner returns code evaluated on fresh instances by both solution quality and execution time. Our central abstraction is a \emph{solver hint}: reusable structure inferred from samples and compiled into specialized solver code. We prove that the empirically fastest sample-consistent solver from a fixed library generalizes in both correctness and runtime, and that statistically identifiable hints can be recovered and compiled from polynomially many samples.
  Empirically, we instantiate the framework with LLM code agents on \(21\) structured combinatorial-optimization target distributions across seven problem classes. The synthesized solvers reach mean normalized quality \(0.971\), improve by \(+0.224\) over the average heuristic pool and by \(+0.098\) over the highest-quality heuristic, and are \(336.9\times\), \(342.8\times\), and \(16.1\times\) faster than the quality-best heuristic, Gurobi, and the selected time-limited exact backend, respectively. On released PACE 2025 Dominating Set private instances, the synthesized solver is valid on all \(100\) graphs and runs about two orders of magnitude faster than top competition solvers, with a moderate quality gap. Inspection shows that many gains come from changing the computational scale: replacing ambient exponential search or general-purpose optimization with compiled distribution-specific computation.

</details>


### [174] [Agentic Systems as Boosting Weak Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14163)
*Varun Sunkaraneni,Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Riccardo Neumarker,Tomaso Poggio,Tomer Galanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了弱推理模型的委员会投票机制是否能达到强模型性能，通过在SWE-bench上使用GPT-5.4 nano模型配合verifier-backed委员会搜索，将性能从67.0%提升到76.4%，匹配了Gemini 3 Pro和Claude Opus 4.5的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以通过组合多个弱推理模型（committee）达到强模型的性能，从而降低对强大单模型能力的依赖。

Method: 提出verifier-backed committee search方法，形式化区分了proposal coverage、local identifiability、progress和diversity四个维度，使用critic-comparator编排机制在隐藏verifier辅助下从弱模型采样中筛选正确解。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，使用GPT-5.4 nano单模型解决67.0%任务；通过critic-comparator编排（k=8）提升至76.4%，匹配Gemini 3 Pro和Claude Opus 4.5 Thinking，接近79.0%的oracle best-of-8上限。

Conclusion: 弱模型采样池中已包含大量正确解，主要挑战在于选择而非生成；剩余失败主要由proposal coverage不足导致（即盲区），仅靠更强的选择机制无法解决。

Abstract: Can a committee of weak reasoning-model calls reach the performance of much stronger models? We study verifier-backed committee search as inference-time boosting for reasoning language models. The mechanism is not simply that ``more agents help'': samples expose latent correct solutions, while critics and comparators must recover them without access to the hidden verifier. We formalize this view by separating proposal coverage, local identifiability, progress, and diversity. We prove that coverage can be amplified by repeated sampling, but cannot by itself create useful critics or comparators; reliable amplification requires an additional local soundness signal, such as execution, proof checking, type checking, tests, or constraint solving. We give rank-based bounds showing when local selection errors compose into reliable trajectories, and characterize the proposer-side ceiling: oracle best-of-\(k\) converges only to the mass of task slices on which the proposal system assigns nonzero useful probability. Empirically, on SWE-bench Verified, a single \texttt{GPT-5.4 nano} proposal solves \(67.0\%\) of tasks. Using the same nano model, our critic--comparator orchestration reaches \(76.4\%\) with \(k=8\) proposals, matching the standalone performance of \texttt{Gemini 3 Pro} and \texttt{Claude Opus 4.5} Thinking and approaching the \(79.0\%\) oracle best-of-\(8\) upper bound. Thus, many correct patches are already present in weak-model proposal pools; the main challenge is selecting them. The remaining failures are mostly proposal-coverage failures, indicating shared blind spots that stronger selection alone cannot close.

</details>


### [175] [Unsteady Metrics and Benchmarking Cultures of AI Model Builders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14164)
*Stefan Baack,Christo Buschek,Maty Bohacek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析2025年11家主要AI公司发布的139个模型所引用的231个基准测试，发现评估体系高度碎片化：63.2%的基准只被一家公司使用，38.5%仅出现一次。基准测试被不同公司赋予不同含义，实质上沦为市场叙事工具而非科学评估手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型能力的宣称方式已从同行评审文献转向公司博客和新闻稿，但哪些基准被选中、传递了什么信息却缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建并开源Benchmarking-Cultures-25数据集，包含139个模型发布中的231个基准；开发统一术语分类体系，将不同公司对基准的描述映射到共同框架；进行定性和定量分析。

Result: 63.2%的基准仅被一家公司使用，38.5%仅出现在一次发布中；不同公司对同一基准赋予不同能力标签；"通用知识应用"是第二受欢迎但定义最模糊的类别，且大多只评估STEM（尤其是数学）领域。

Conclusion: 当前基准测试主要作为灵活的市场叙事工具而非标准化科学评估手段，优先服务于市场定位而非科学评价。

Abstract: The primary way to establish and compare competencies in foundation and generative AI models has shifted from peer-reviewed literature to press releases and company blog posts, where model builders highlight results on selected benchmarks. These artifacts now largely define the state of the art for researchers and the public. Despite their prominence, which benchmarks model builders choose to highlight, and what they communicate through this selection, is underexamined. To investigate, we introduce and open-source Benchmarking-Cultures-25, a dataset of 231 benchmarks highlighted across 139 model releases in 2025 from 11 major AI builders, alongside an interactive tool to explore the data. Our analysis reveals a fragmented evaluation landscape with limited cross-model comparability: 63.2% of highlighted benchmarks are used by a single builder, and 38.5% appear in just one release. Few achieve widespread use (e.g., GPQA Diamond, LiveCodeBench, AIME 2025). Moreover, benchmarks are attributed different competencies by different builders, depending on their narrative. To disentangle these conflicting presentations, we develop a unified taxonomy mapping diverging terminology to a shared framework of measured signals based on what benchmark authors claim to measure. "General knowledge application" is the second most popular, yet vaguely defined, category. Qualitative analysis shows many such benchmarks deemphasize construct validity, instead framing results as indicators of progress toward AGI. Their authors claim to measure knowledge or reasoning broadly, yet mostly evaluate STEM subjects (especially math). We argue that highlighted benchmarks function less as standardized measurement tools and more as flexible narrative devices prioritizing market positioning over scientific evaluation. Data: https://hf.co/datasets/matybohacek/benchmarking-cultures-25; tool: https://bench-cultures.net.

</details>


### [176] [The Evaluation Trap: Benchmark Design as Theoretical Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14167)
*Theodore J Kalaitzidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示AI基准测试因固守未经检验的理论假设而陷入自证循环，并提出Epistematics方法论用于审计基准测试与能力主张之间的逻辑一致性，通过对Dupoux等人(2026)的案例分析展示该方法如何识别评估框架中隐蔽的范式约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI基准测试将理论假设视为理所当然，导致评估标准窄化、能力概念被重构，最终形成自我强化的"评估陷阱"——既掩盖了当前范式的结构性局限，又无法真正衡量所声称的能力。

Method: 提出Epistematics方法论，包括：(1)从技术能力主张中推导评估标准；(2)审计基准测试能否区分真正能力与代理行为（捷径/伪迹）；(3)提供失效模式分类法和基准设计标准。

Result: 通过对Dupoux等人(2026)的工作审计，展示了该方法如何识别出一个关键矛盾：该工作在架构层面修正了主流范式的理论假设，却在评估标准中保留了相同假设，从而使评估无法检测到其意图克服的约束。

Conclusion: 评估框架需要一种元评价（meta-evaluative）视角，即对评价本身进行批判性审视，否则基准测试将不再追踪独立的能力目标，而是不断再生产自身假设下的狭隘"进步"。

Abstract: Every AI benchmark operationalizes theoretical assumptions about the capability it claims to assess. When assumptions function as unexamined commitments, benchmarks stabilize the dominant paradigm by narrowing what counts as progress. Over time, narrow evaluation reorganizes capability concepts: architectures and definitions are selected for benchmark legibility until evaluation ceases to track an independent object and instead produces a version of the target defined by its own operational assumptions. The result is a trap: evaluation frameworks treat self-reinforcing assessments as valid, both creating and obscuring structural limits on what the current paradigm can accomplish. We introduce Epistematics, a methodology for deriving evaluation criteria directly from technical capability claims and auditing whether proposed benchmarks can discriminate the claimed capability from proxy behaviors. The contribution is meta-evaluative: an audit procedure, a failure mode taxonomy, and benchmark-design criteria for evaluating capability-evaluation coherence. We demonstrate the procedure through a worked audit of Dupoux et al. (2026), a proposal that revises the dominant paradigm's theoretical assumptions at the architectural level while reproducing them in its evaluation criteria, thereby entrenching the constraint it seeks to overcome in a form the evaluation cannot detect.

</details>


### [177] [Grounded Continuation: A Linear-Time Runtime Verifier for LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14175)
*Qisong He,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个运行时验证器，通过显式依赖图跟踪对话中每个声明的证据支持，实现线性成本的撤回传播和形式化无冲突保证，在多个基准测试上超越纯LLM基线（准确率最高+6.7pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长对话中LLM可能基于已废弃前提生成看似合理的话语，且上下文操纵攻击利用这一漏洞，现有方法缺乏形式化的前提跟踪与撤回机制。

Method: 运行时验证器：LLM将每轮对话分类为8种更新操作（来自动态认知逻辑、溯因推理、意识逻辑、论证理论四种形式化体系），符号引擎构建显式依赖图，通过图遍历检查支持关系，撤回通过同一图传播。

Result: LongMemEval-KU oracle达到89.7%准确率（超过LLM基线+1.3pp，超过RAG基线+2.6pp）；LoCoMo上与RAG基线持平；自建50项接地测试中，过时前提子集100%准确率（+6.7pp）。撤回检查微秒级，历史回放线性增长。

Conclusion: 提出了可靠性-忠实性分解框架：结构检查具有形式化可靠性保证，LLM提取的忠实性作为可实证衡量的维度，验证器在效率和准确性上均优于纯LLM方法。

Abstract: In long conversations, an LLM can produce a next utterance that sounds plausible but rests on premises the conversation has already abandoned. Context-manipulation attacks against deployed agents now actively exploit this gap. We close it with a runtime verifier that maintains an explicit dependency graph: an LLM classifies each turn into one of 8 update operations drawn from four formalisms (dynamic epistemic logic, abductive reasoning, awareness logic, argumentation), and a symbolic engine records which claims depend on which evidence. Checking whether a continuation is supported reduces to a graph walk; retraction propagates through the same graph to flag exactly the conclusions that lose support, with linear per-turn cost and a formal conflict-free guarantee. On LongMemEval-KU oracle (n=78), the verifier reaches 89.7% accuracy vs. 88.5% for the LLM-only baseline (+1.3pp) and 87.2% for a transcript-RAG baseline matched on retrieval budget (+2.6pp); wins among disagreements are correct abstentions where the baseline confabulates. On LoCoMo's 60 official QA items the verifier is competitive with retrieval-augmented baselines. Beyond external benchmarks, we construct two multi-agent scenarios and a 50-item grounding test: on the 15-item stale-premise subset, the verifier reaches 100% accuracy vs. 93.3% (+6.7pp). These instantiate a soundness-faithfulness decomposition: the structural check is sound by construction, and per-deployment LLM extraction faithfulness is the empirical question we measure across four LLM families. The retraction check plateaus at microseconds while history-replay grows linearly with conversation length.

</details>


### [178] [SimPersona: Learning Discrete Buyer Personas from Raw Clickstreams for Grounded E-Commerce Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14205)
*Zahra Zanjani Foumani,Alberto Castelo,Shuang Xie,Ted Chaiwachirasak,Han Li,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SimPersona是一个从历史点击流中学习离散买家类型、将其作为角色令牌注入LLM智能体的框架，在42个电商平台上对837万买家实现了78%的转化率对齐，显著优于传统基于提示词的角色方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM网页智能体在模拟买家行为时倾向于退化为单一的"平均买家"策略，而基于手工提示词的角色个性化方法脆弱、难以扩展、上下文效率低，且无法忠实反映真实买家群体层面的行为分布。

Method: 使用行为感知的VQ-VAE从原始点击流中学习离散买家类型空间，将每种买家类型映射为LLM智能体词汇表中的专用角色令牌，然后用真实浏览轨迹对有此类令牌的智能体进行微调；推理时通过单次编码器前向传播分配买家类型，群体模拟时从经验分布中采样。

Result: 在42个电商平台的837万买家上实现78%的转化率对齐，展现出可解释的跨买家类型行为差异，在目标导向购物任务上超越参数量8倍以上的基线模型。

Conclusion: SimPersona通过离散买家类型学习和角色令牌注入，有效解决了LLM智能体在电商模拟中忽视买家异质性的问题，同时开源了从原始电商日志到买家表征的数据流水线。

Abstract: LLM-based web agents can navigate live storefronts, yet they often collapse to a single "average buyer" policy, failing to capture the heterogeneous and distributional nature of real buyer populations. Existing personalization methods rely on hand-crafted prompt-based personas that are brittle, difficult to scale, context-inefficient, and unable to faithfully represent population-level behavior. We introduce SimPersona, a novel framework that learns discrete buyer types from historical traffic and exposes them to LLM-based web agents as compact persona tokens. Given raw clickstreams, a behavior-aware VQ-VAE induces a discrete buyer-type space that captures the statistical structure of real buyer behavior and merchant-specific buyer population distributions. To provide behavior-specific guidance to LLM-based web agents, SimPersona maps each learned buyer type to a dedicated persona token in the LLM agent vocabulary and fine-tunes the agent with these tokens on real browsing traces. At inference, each synthetic buyer is assigned to a learned buyer type with a single encoder forward pass, requiring no retraining or store-specific prompt engineering. For population-level simulation, SimPersona samples buyer types from each merchant's empirical distribution over the learned VQ-VAE codebook and instantiates agents with the corresponding persona tokens, preserving merchant-specific buyer population distributions.
